{"forms":[{"form":"more precipitously","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most precipitously","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"In the west of Ireland, the Cliffs of Moher drop precipitously into the North Atlantic.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a precipitous manner.","Abruptly, perilously, sharply, steeply, severely."],"raw_tags":["of changes within physical environments"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Shortly after the conversation turned to the results of the recent election, John silently stood from the table, and without explanation, precipitously walked out on the dinner party.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a precipitous manner.","Abruptly; with no notice or ceremony: in a sudden or hasty manner, instantly, quickly."],"raw_tags":["of events in general"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In signing that contract without reading all of the provisions, I have performed what was a legal requirement most precipitously, leading me to wish that I had taken more care.","type":"example"},{"text":"Look here son, in stealing that candy bar from Belsito's market, you have acted precipitously in the extreme; now you will have the unenviable responsibility of telling Mr. Belsito what you have done, and of expressing your heartfelt regret.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a precipitous manner.","Done with a lack of proper decorum, or without adequate thought, care or concern for consequences."],"raw_tags":["of actions involving intentionality"],"synonyms":[{"word":"carelessly"},{"word":"impulsively"},{"word":"rashly"},{"word":"rash"}]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/pɹɪˈsɪ.pɪ.təs.li/","tags":["US"]}],"upstream_line":11018,"upstream_offset":68606064,"upstream_record_sha256":"940ae0c3cb12f4a9310976f206825e53ee1c73b807e6cec832188e223557b115","word":"precipitously"}
